# JAMB missing-content packet — complete this document only

This is the complete manual source packet for the JAMB admission layer.
Everything to inspect is listed below. Do not open any repository report or
Markdown report to find the item. Open only the original brochure PDFs in:

`C:\Users\USER\Downloads\jamb brochure`

Copy the relevant text from the PDF into the `YOUR SOURCE RESPONSE`
line under each item. Include the PDF page and table row. Do not complete
CCMAS, domain, subgroup or role mapping in this packet.

## What is already complete

- 420 effective JAMB programme names are already present.
- 9,129 university/programme offering pairs are already present.
- 3,883 university-specific exception records are preserved.
- 2,030 university-specific subject options are already structured.
- Do not re-enter an offering unless you find that the school/programme pair is actually missing or wrong.

## How to read every item

For a national item, copy the complete `REQUIREMENTS` or `UTME SUBJECTS`
cell, including continuation rows and shared headings.

For a university item, search the PDF for the programme name and the
institution together, then copy the complete `SPECIAL CONSIDERATION
(WAIVER) REMARKS` text or the relevant requirement cell.

Never turn a Direct Entry qualification into a UTME subject. If a cell is
genuinely blank, write `SOURCE IS BLANK` rather than guessing.

Use this response format under every item:

```text
YOUR SOURCE RESPONSE:
PDF PAGE:
TABLE ROW:
BROCHURE TEXT:
DECISION: exact rule | shared rule | partial condition | cross-reference | source blank | direct entry only
REFERENCE: programme named by the brochure, if any
```

## Part 1 — national requirement cells

There are **170 national source-review layer records**. These are grouped by the PDF page and row already identified by the audit. Resolve each item below.

brochure proves that an existing school/programme pair is wrong or that
a school/programme pair is absent:

## Finish condition

This manual pass is complete when every N-item and U-item has either a
copied brochure rule or an explicit `SOURCE IS BLANK` response, and any
offering correction has a PDF page and row. The parser and audits will be
rerun after the responses are supplied.
